# Chart campaign: LOInK in place of the IKFlow chart

Generated by `python scripts/report_loink.py --write`; numbers only. Rows are the record's experiments (robot x task x start), 480 cells each, 180 s, seed 1, one solve per GPU. **flow** = the record's learned arm (IPOPT: stage PAPER, Panda stage SVGD_R2; AL-SVGD: SVGD_R29 `pin4`; screw: SCREWREDO `n6r` where it exists); **JS** = the record's joint-space arm (IPOPT: the same runs; AL-SVGD: SVGD_R30 `pin4kq`). Charts are `loink<cost><act><recipe>`: cost m / x / u, activation r (relu) / t (tanh), recipe P (batch 4096, 620000 steps) / A (batch 500, 150000 steps); K = DOF - 6 (Panda 1, iiwa 1, screw 1, soft PCS 6), trust region round(sqrt(K) + 1.5, 2). Every chart run is refused unless its grid hash, scene fingerprint, cap and cells are the record row's and its checkpoint is the chart its tag names. `not yet measured` = no merged chart run collected. `chart does not fit (K = 1)` (every Panda, iiwa and screw chart) and `recipe A: LR collapsed` (soft PCS recipe A) mark the 42 rungs cancelled after their training wave, never to be measured; mechanism in `docs/notes-chart.md`, "Training outcome (2026-10-23)". The single-branch charts `loinkB...` (phase 2) have their own blocks per solver, the branch stated there. `x/{PROBE_TARGETS}` = the {PROBE_TARGETS}-cell PROBE (guess 0 of the first {PROBE_TARGETS} targets), shown where the row has no full run; its pairings, costs and medians are over those 20 cells only. McNemar cells: `chart-only / other-only, p` on the discordant cells (exact two-sided). Cost cells: `median per-cell ratio chart / other [95% bootstrap CI over targets, 1000 resamples] (n shared cells)`, on cells both arms of the pair solved, `--` under 10; the reported cost, learned-only regularisers excluded. Cap check: `clock` / `budget` = cells stopped by the wall clock / an iteration budget (>= 24 at a budget is flagged **VOID**).

### IPOPT, single branch (B)

**Single branch (B)** (phase 2, 2026-10-24): the Panda and iiwa charts retrained on ONE IK branch, the nominal configuration's, by the analytic classifiers of `src/branches.py` -- Panda wrist B1 / shoulder C2 / elbow A +1 (`gc` (1, 2, +1)); iiwa signs (+1, -1, +1) of q2 / q4 / q6 -- labels `loinkB<cost><act><recipe>`, K = 1, trust region 2.5. The arm can only return configurations on that branch: a target reachable only on another branch is lost by construction, and a paired-protocol cell whose start lies off the branch may be scored `unrepresentable_start` (the `unrep` diagnostic column counts them). The screw arm has no branch function (`no branch function`) and the soft PCS charts are K = 6 and not retrained (`n/a (K = 6, all branches)`).

### AL-SVGD, single branch (B)

**Single branch (B)** (phase 2, 2026-10-24): the Panda and iiwa charts retrained on ONE IK branch, the nominal configuration's, by the analytic classifiers of `src/branches.py` -- Panda wrist B1 / shoulder C2 / elbow A +1 (`gc` (1, 2, +1)); iiwa signs (+1, -1, +1) of q2 / q4 / q6 -- labels `loinkB<cost><act><recipe>`, K = 1, trust region 2.5. The arm can only return configurations on that branch: a target reachable only on another branch is lost by construction, and a paired-protocol cell whose start lies off the branch may be scored `unrepresentable_start` (the `unrep` diagnostic column counts them). The screw arm has no branch function (`no branch function`) and the soft PCS charts are K = 6 and not retrained (`n/a (K = 6, all branches)`).

## Acceptance: joint space after the chart refactor

`sc_LOINKACC_panda_n6_ipopt_posetip_480_180_paired` (joint space only, the record's flags) against the record's joint-space arm on Panda pose paired under IPOPT: per cell, q, iterations, cost and verdict must be identical (a cell the clock stopped in both runs need only agree on the verdict).

IDENTICAL -- of the 479 cells not stopped by the clock in both runs: q 479, iterations 479, cost 479, feasible 479; of the 1 clock-stopped in both, verdict identical on 1 (record run `sc_SVGD_R2_panda_n6_ipopt_posetip_480_180_paired`).
